- Plane-Touched Wings. You have feathered wings sprouting from your shoulder blades. You have a flying speed of 30 feet while not wearing heavy armor. This trait replaces the Light Bearer trait and the Radiant Soul, Radiant Consumption, or Necrotic Shroud trait as applicable.

# Bastions

Jump to
Basic Facilities	2418	2422
A starting Bastion must contain two Basic Facilities (one Cramped and one Roomy) and two Special Facilities. You must be level 5+ and meet all other prerequisites. See Appendix B of the TBD Guidelines for details.
Any facility or component built as part of a Bastion can be torn down and 50% of its gold cost refunded. The Bastion must still contain at least two Basic Facilities (including one Cramped and one Roomy) and two Special Facilities afterward to be useable.
A Special Facility can be replaced with another Special Facility by paying the gold and DTP to build the new facility, but the full gold cost of the old facility contributes to the new one
You can combine your Bastion with Bastions built by one or more characters of other players into a single structure as written in the 2024 Dungeon Master's Guide.  Each participant can mutually contribute gold and DTP in building and expanding Bastions in this way.
Any items acquired or crafted from Bastions are subject to the normal trading rules (including needing to wait 30 days before self trading)

Special Facilities
You must meet all prerequisites of a Special Facility in order to build and use it. The maximum number of Special Facilities your Bastion can have depends on your character's level (see Appendix B of the TBD Guidelines for details)
A Bastion can't have more than one Special Facility of the same kind unless the facility's description says otherwise.
A Special Facility can be replaced with another Special Facility by paying the gold and DTP to build the new facility, but the full gold cost of the old facility contributes to the new one
Only the Bastion's owner can benefit from its Special Facilities except where explicitly noted otherwise.
Certain Special Facilities have associated Craftable Magic Item Types to be used with the Crafting Magic Item downtime activity

# Loot

Jump to
Rolled Loot	3104	3108	3111
T0      T1      T2      T3      T4	3120	3122	3124
The listed Base Cost of an item does not mean that the magic item can be purchased or sold. Only items as listed in Equipment can be purchased or sold.     
Event items can only be acquired during the event they were created for. DM Select items can only be awarded by a specific DM and other DMs collaborating with them with their permission.
Name	24	Type	Tier	Description	92
Rolled Loot
Rolled Loot can be rolled in #treasure-log using /hgenloot [Number of Players] [APL]. A DM can also choose to predetermine rolled loot instead of rolling, with a number of item slots = the number of players.
 For predetermined loot, no more than half the items (rounded down) for a game can be permanents and the maximum item tier is up to the tier for the APL. Trial DMs can only predetermine up to T2 Rolled Loot.
A DM can choose to predetermine some of the items and roll for the remainder.
T0 Items (APL 3+)
DMs can add up to a number of predetermined T0 items to a game up to half the number of players (rounded down), and only one of them can be a permanent.
T0 items don't count against the normal number of item slots for a game.
T0 Consumables (Go to T0 Permanents)
DMs can predetermine up to 4 duplicates of a T0 consumable per item slot (up to 15 for ammunition)

For APL 17+ games, the DM can add bonus loot by rolling loot at APL 4 and selecting either 1 T1 permanent or up to 2 T2 consumables from the list. (The DM can also choose to predetermine the same instead for either T1 Select Loot or Rolled Loot)
T1 Consumables (Go to T1 Permanents)
DMs can predetermine up to 3 duplicates of a T1 consumable per item slot (up to 20 for ammunition)

Select Loot
Select Loot can only be predetermined by a DM, not rolled.
 For predetermined loot, no more than half the items (rounded down) for a game can be permanents and the maximum item tier is up to the tier for the APL. Trial DMs can only predetermine up to T2 Select Loot.
A DM can choose to predetermine some Select Loot items and roll for the remainder.
T1 Select Items (APL 3+)
For APL 17+ games, the DM can add bonus loot by rolling loot at APL 4 and selecting either 1 T1 permanent or up to 2 T2 consumables from the list. (The DM can also choose to predetermine the same instead for either T1 Select Loot or Rolled Loot)

Multisession Loot
Multisession loot is predetermined loot awarded after a multisession with a listed minimum APL and number of sessions, but must always be at least 3+ sessions. Only Full DMs can drop multisession loot.
Multisession loot doesn't count against the number of item slots for the session it is awarded in; it is awarded in addition to those items as a reward for the multisession as a whole.
Each type of multisession is on its own separate track: for example, an APL 11+ multisession of 3+ sessions could award both a T2+ and a T3+ item.
Multisession loot cannot be crafted.
T1+ Items (APL 1+)
T1+ items are multisession loot that requires a multisession of 3+ sessions at APL 1+ to award and can only be awarded by Full DMs.
T1+ Consumables (Go to T1+ Permanents)
See the notes below for how many duplicates of a T1+ consumable can be awarded for a APL 1+ multisession of 3+ sessions
At this time, there are no T1+ Consumables.

At this time, there are no T1+ Permanents.

Relics of Emergence
Relics of Emergence are unique homebrew multisession loot that use the Vestiges of Divergence system from Explorers Guide to Wildemount.
Relics of Emergence are evolving items that respond to a specific wielder and that change between 3 states with growing abilities and powers: Dormant, Awakened, and Exalted.
As homebrew items, a Full DM must submit a Rules request detailing the Relic of Emergence for approval, including its statistics for each state and may be asked to give an outline on how it is meant to be acquired and upgraded.
The tier of each state of a homebrew Relic of Emergence is determined collaboratively by the Full DM and the Rules team.
An approved Relic of Emergence follows the normal item distribution rules with one change: they are multisession items that require 3+ sessions to drop or upgrade, which can only be done by Full DMs.
An approved Relic of Emergence can only ever be initially acquired in its Dormant state as part of a multisession. Upgrading a Relic of Emergence into an Awakened and later Exalted state requires separate multisessions by a Full DM.
An acquired Relic of Emergence can be upgraded in a multisession ran by a different Full DM than the one that originally dropped it, provided they have the original Full DM’s consent and access to the original request.
The full statistics of a Relic of Emergence are known only to the original Full DM and the staff teams, as well as any DMs they collaborate with. A player learns the item's statistics only as it reveals more of its power by evolving.
Relics of Emergence are almost always unique: only one of that specific kind of Relic of Emergence will ever exist, as homebrewed by a Full DM and approved by the Lore and Rules teams.

Other Loot
Deck of Many Things
The Deck of Many Things (DMG2014/2024), the Deck of Many More Things (BMT), and the Deck of Wonder (BMT) cannot be acquired by PCs but a DM can choose to incorporate and use them in an adventure.
Any lasting effects of a Deck are localized to the adventure only and they expire and reverse themselves in a narratively coherent fashion at the end of an adventure.
PCs can find and acquire individual cards of the Deck of Many Things as awarded by DMs. These cards are awarded as Select or Multisession permanent items as listed below.
To use individual cards, the PC must assemble cards they own into a personalized deck after finishing a Long Rest. A personalized deck must contain no duplicates and the maximum deck size is equal to the PC's Proficiency Bonus.
Individual cards can't be used outside of a personalized deck and a PC can only benefit from and use cards from a personalized deck that they make. A PC can only have one personalized deck at a time.
These items cannot be crafted.

Hoard Magic Items
Hoard Magic Items are loot from Fizban’s Treasury of Dragons. Similar to Relics of Emergence, they exist in different states and can grow in power and evolve.
Hoard Magic Items exist in 4 states: Slumbering, Stirring, Wakened, and Ascendant. As described in Fizban's Treasury of Dragons, they can grow in power by one state after being steeped in the hoard of a recently slain dragon.
Each state is associated with a draconic age category: Slumbering for dragons of any age, Stirring for young dragons, Wakened for adult dragons, and Ascendant for ancient dragons.
A Hoard Magic Item steeped in the hoard of a recently slain dragon can't grow beyond the state associated with the dragon's age. For example, steeping a Wakened item in a slain adult dragon's hoard doesn't make it Ascendant.
Hoard Magic Items can be acquired in states higher than Slumbering, as either from a dragon's hoard of appropriate age category or as wielded by others.
Unlike Relics of Emergence, awarding or upgrading Hoard Magic Items follows the normal item distribution rules and can only be awarded or upgraded by Full DMs.
A Hoard Magic Item with the damage resistance Draconic Quirk only grants the benefit of resistance in its Wakened and Ascendant states.
If a character that possesses a Hoard Magic Item trades it or undergoes a checkpoint rework into a different character, the item reverts to its Slumbering state.

Superior Ship Upgrades
Superior Ship Upgrades are upgrades for ships as described in Ghosts of Saltmarsh. Each listed ship upgrade can be awarded as a Select permanent item of the associated tier.
Ship upgrades can only be awarded in a session where at least one or more of the player characters owns an eligible air, space, or water vehicle.
Except where written otherwise, ship upgrades can be installed onto air, space, and water vehicles as listed in Equipment. Ship upgrades cannot be crafted.
Ship upgrades can only be installed while the vehicle is berthed. Installing a ship upgrade costs 20 DTP and 15,000 GP.
Installed ship upgrades cannot be removed or traded. However, if another ship upgrade is installed on the same component of the ship, it replaces any preexisting upgrade on that component.
A ship can only ever have one hull upgrade and one figurehead upgrade respectively. A weapon on a ship can only ever have one upgrade, and a weapon upgrade only affects one weapon on a ship.
A movement component on a ship (such as sails or oars) can only ever have one movement upgrade per movement type (such as sails or oars).
A ship can have any number of miscellaneous upgrades but can only gain a specific miscellaneous upgrade once.

Unlisted Official Items
Other items that have been officially printed are not present here. There are various reasons why an item might have been omitted, which can include but is not limited to the reasons listed below.
a) The item is unique to an official adventure and has been left to be acquired in that adventure if a DM runs it b) the item is a unique item (such as artifacts) for which multiple copies can't be distributed by multiple DMs c) the item was deemed not to fit well with the server environment as a shared persistent world (such as the Deck of Many Things) or can readily facilitate problematic patterns of gameplay and behavior (such as the Philter of Love)

Dark Gifts
Dark Gifts are a type of Supernatural Reward that consist of a mixture of beneficial and detrimental abilities due to contact with dark supernatural forces. They cannot be traded or sold.
Dark Gifts do not replace an item in the session. A DM can give a character or multiple characters a Dark Gift at their discretion due to events in an adventure.
A character can only ever have one Dark Gift. If a character receives another Dark Gift, they must choose to keep the old Dark Gift or lose it and replace it with the new one.

Supernatural Gifts
Supernatural Gifts are a type of Supernatural Reward that consist of extraordinary permanent abilities acquired after great deeds or contact with powerful supernatural forces. They cannot be traded or sold.
Supernatural Gifts follow the normal item distribution rules and are treated as permanent items and can only be awarded by Full DMs.
A character can have multiple Supernatural Gifts, but can only benefit from a number whose combined tier is no greater than their Proficiency Bonus. They choose which combination of Supernatural Gifts if any they have active whenever they finish a Long Rest.

Mythic Gifts (Tier S)
Mythic Gifts are a type of Supernatural Reward that consist of extraordinary permanent abilities only available to level 20 characters. They cannot be traded or sold.
Mythic Gifts are Tier S rewards: they are multisession rewards that require 8+ sessions at APL 20 to award and can only be awarded by Full DMs.
A character can have multiple Mythic Gifts, but can only benefit from one at a time. They choose which Mythic Gift if any they have active whenever they finish a Long Rest.

Boon of Spell Recall	3209	Mythic Gift (Supernatural Reward)	Tier S	Mythic Gift. See book for description.
Charms
Charms are a type of Supernatural Reward that consist of minor abilities that typically have limited uses similar to consumable items. They cannot be traded or sold.
Charms follow the normal item distribution rules and are treated as consumable items (only 1 charm per item slot except where otherwise stated)
A character can have any number of charms, but can't benefit from the same charm more than once: a character receiving the same charm again only regains any expended uses of the charm.

T0 charms can take up a session's T0 item allotment, with 1 charm per T0 slot

Mounts
A mount is a creature that can be used by a PC as a mount following the rules for mounted combat.
The mounts listed below follow the normal item distribution rules and are treated as permanent items (only 1 mount per item slot)
Sapient mounts such as nightmares or unicorns cannot be traded and they can only be used as controlled mounts at a DM's discretion.
A sapient mount's name, alignment, and personality must be written in the session log in which it is acquired.
A DM can use the guidelines on customizing monsters in the DM Guidelines for mounts they award.
A DM can choose to award a mount that has been awakened (such as by the Awaken spell) but the mount is then considered to be a sapient mount.

Pets
Pets can't take the Attack or Help actions and normally can't participate in adventures: a pet only participates in adventures otherwise with a DM's express permission and at their discretion
A DM can submit a Rules request to award a creature not listed here as a vanity pet
Pets can also be awarded as the equivalent of a T0 Permanent item and follow the associated item distribution rules for doing so 
A DM can use the guidelines on customizing monsters in the DM Guidelines for pets they award.
Sapient pets cannot be traded or sold.                                                
A sapient pet's name, alignment, and personality must be written in the session log in which it is acquired.
A DM can choose to award a pet that has been awakened (such as by the Awaken spell) but the mount is then considered to be a sapient pet.
Name	24	Type	Tier	Description	92

Renown
At character creation, a character can start with a Renown score of 3 as a respected member of a group in the setting. If the group is listed below, they start with any associated Renown Perks
If the chosen group requires collaborating with a specific DM, the player first needs that DM's permission to be a member of the group at character creation.

Harpers (Go to Renown)
Name	24	Type	Tier	Description	92
Harpers	4885	Group (Faction)	—	See Forgotten Realms: Heroes of Faerûn for a description, list of Renown rewards, and member benefits. See other sources for additional information.

The Harpers is a scattered network of spellcasters and spies who advocate equality and covertly oppose the abuse of power. The organization is benevolent, knowledgeable, and secretive. Bards and wizards of good alignments are commonly drawn to the Harpers.

Motto: Down with tyranny. Fairness and equality for all.

Goals
- Gather information throughout Faerûn.
- Promote fairness and equality by covert means.
- Thwart tyrants and leaders, governments, and organizations that grow too powerful.
- Aid the weak, poor and oppressed.

Beliefs
- One can never have too much information or arcane knowledge.
- Too much power leads to corruption. The abuse of magic must be closely monitored.
- No one should be powerless.

Ranks
- Watcher: Renown Score 3+
- Harpshadow: Renown Score 15+
- Brightcandle: Renown Score 25+
- Wise Owl: Renown Score 40+
- High Harper: Renown Score 50+	4891
Harper Token (HB)	4895	Wondrous Item	T0	Wondrous Item, T0

This silver brooch is embossed with the symbol of the Harpers, depicting a harp against a crescent moon. This brooch is presented to newly inducted members of the Harpers to serve as faction insignia as a token of their membership.

While wearing this brooch, you gain the following benefit.

Telepathic Detection. The pin grows warm if a creature within 30 feet of yourself attempts to use telepathy to communicate.	4900
Lesser Harper Pin (HB)	4895	Wondrous Item	T1 Select	Wondrous Item, T1 (Requires Attunement)

This silver brooch is embossed with the symbol of the Harpers, depicting a harp against a crescent moon. This brooch is presented to dedicated members among the Harpers.

While wearing this pin, you gain the following benefits.

Mind Shielding. You are immune to magic that allows other creatures to read your thoughts, determine whether you are lying, know your alignment, or know your creature type. Creatures can telepathically communicate with you only if you allow it.

Telepathic Detection. The pin grows warm if a creature within 30 feet of yourself attempts to use telepathy to communicate.	4906
Harpspeak	4895	Story Outcome	T0	Story Outcome, T0

You've proven yourself to be a reliable Harper agent. You learn Harpspeak, a secret non-verbal language and means of communication known only to Harpers. Harpspeak can be used either in the form of a sign language or with scribed runes to leave hidden messages. 

As a sign language, Harpspeak uses ordinary gestures in patterns that allow for messages to be hidden in seemingly normal and innocuous gestures such as smiles, frowns, and winks. Only a creature that knows Harpspeak understands such messages. It takes four times longer to convey such a message than it does to speak the same idea plainly. 

As scribed runes, Harpspeak can be used to leave behind hidden messages for other Harpers. Creatures who know Harpspeak automatically spot such messages. Others spot the message's presence with a successful DC 15 Wisdom (Perception) check but can't decipher it without magic. Harpers sometimes mark the interior of these runes with extra dots to leave behind a misleading message for their enemies and to alert their fellow Harpers that the true symbol and its meaning is somewhere else nearby.	4910
Chord of Calling	4895	Story Outcome	T0	Story Outcome, T0

You are a trusted voice among the Harpers and a pillar of communities, recognized finding and rooting out injustice where you can. During adventures in Faerûn, you can spend 7 days to call upon major aid from the Harpers and obtain assistance from Harper spellcasters or other support. The nature of this assistance is determined by the DM.	4920
Greater Harper Pin (HB)	4895	Wondrous Item	T4 Select	Wondrous Item, T4 (Requires Attunement)

This silver brooch is embossed with the symbol of the Harpers, depicting a harp against a crescent moon. This brooch is presented to those that have become a High Harper, the highest rank in the leadership of the Harpers. Unlike the Harper pin provided earlier in a Harper's career, the silver of this pin has been magically treated to be as hard as adamantine and has extraordinary properties.

While wearing this pin, you gain the following effects..

Mind Shielding. You have Advantage on saving throws made to avoid or end the Charmed or Frightened condition. Additionally, you are immune to magic that allows other creatures to read your thoughts, determine whether you are lying, know your alignment, or know your creature type. Creatures can telepathically communicate with you only if you allow it.

Nondetection. The pin has the properties of an Amulet of Proof Against Detection and Location and the pin itself is also protected against such effects.

Resilience. You have Resistance to Lightning damage and you have Immunity to damage from the Magic Missile spell.

Telepathic Detection. The pin grows warm if a creature within 30 feet of yourself attempts to use telepathy to communicate.	4929
Order of the Gauntlet (Go to Renown)
Name	24	Type	Tier	Description	92
Order of the Gauntlet	4942	Group (Faction)	—	See Forgotten Realms: Heroes of Faerûn for a description, list of Renown rewards, and member benefits. See other sources for additional information.

The Order of the Gauntlet is composed of faithful and vigilant seekers of justice who protect others from the depredations of evildoers. The organization is honorable, vigilant, and zealous. Clerics, monks, and paladins of good (and often lawful good) alignments are commonly drawn to the Order of the Gauntlet.

Motto: The gods are with us. When evil strikes, we strike back.

Goals
- Be armed and vigilant against evil.
- Identify evil threats such as secretive power groups and inherently evil creatures.
- Enforce justice.
- Enact retribution against evil actions—do not strike preemptively.

Beliefs
- Faith is the greatest weapon against evil—faith in one’s god, one’s friends, and one’s self.
- Battling evil is an extraordinary task that requires extraordinary strength and bravery.
- Punishing an evil act is just. Punishing an evil thought is not.

Ranks
- Chevall: Renown Score 3+
- Marcheon: Renown Score 15+
- Whitehawk: Renown Score 25+
- Vindicator: Renown Score 40+
- Righteous Hand: Renown Score 50+

Titles
- Knight of the Guard: Renown Score 5+
- Knight of the Banner: Renown Score 15+
- Knight of Justice: Renown Score 30+
- Chapter Knight: Renown Score 50+	4949
Emblem of the Order (HB)	4895	Wondrous Item	T0	Wondrous Item, T0

This Emblem serves as faction insignia given to new members of the Order of the Gauntlet and to mark them as belonging to the faction. Prominently displayed upon the Emblem is the symbol of the Order of the Gauntlet: a gauntleted fist grasping a sword by the blade.

You can use this Emblem as a Holy Symbol. While wearing the fabric or holding the Shield that the Emblem is borne upon, you can take a Bonus Action to cause the Emblem to shed Bright Light in a 10-foot radius and Dim Light for an additional 10 feet, or to extinguish the light.	4956
Hero's Welcome	4895	Story Outcome	T0	Story Outcome, T0

Word of your deeds among the Order of the Gauntlet have spread among the common folk of the Sword Coast and beyond as a champion of justice. 

You can find a place to hide, rest, or recuperate among other commoners, unless you have shown yourself to be a danger to them. They will shield you from the law or anyone else searching for you and will extend you every courtesy and do their utmost to help you. Unless you have shown yourself to be a danger to them, they will even take up arms to fight alongside you, should you find yourself facing an enemy alone.	4962
Hippogriffs of the Hawk	4895	Story Outcome	T0	Story Outcome, T0

You have proven your valor and integrity and that you can be counted as a trusted member of the Order. You have earned the right to acquire Hippogriffs from the Hawk's Nest, a fortification near Silverymoon Pass protected by the Order of the Gauntlet. 

While in Faerûn, you can expend 30 DTP and 1000 GP to purchase a Hippogriff as a downtime activity; the Hippogriff is delivered to the settlement or stronghold nearest to you where the Order of the Gauntlet is present.	4973
Rally the Order	4895	Story Outcome	T0	Story Outcome, T0

You are a champion of the Order of the Gauntlet, with your actions recognized to have brought glory, honor, and respect to the Order and its ideals. During adventures in Faerûn, you can spend 7 days to call upon major aid from the Order of the Gauntlet and obtain assistance from Order spellcasters or other support. The nature of this assistance is determined by the DM.	4979
Talisman of the Gauntlet (HB)	4895	Wondrous Item	T4 Select	Wondrous Item, T4 (Requires Attunement)

This silver talisman is emblazoned with the symbol of the Order of the Gauntlet, depicting the blade of retribution held by the gauntlet of justice. This talisman is presented to those that have become a Righteous Hand, the highest rank in the Order of the Gauntlet's leadership. 

Holy Symbol. You can use the talisman as a Holy Symbol. You gain a +3 bonus to your saving throws while you wear or hold this talisman.

Holy Rebuke. This talisman has 7 charges and regains 1d6 + 1 expended charges daily at dawn. While holding or wearing this talisman, you can take a Magic action to expend 1 or more of its charges to cause one of the following effects:
- Rebuke Evil. You expend 1 charge and target one creature you can see within 60 feet of yourself. The target must succeed on a DC 17 Wisdom saving throw, taking 8d6 Psychic damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one. If the target is evil, it has Disadvantage on the save. 
- Turn Evil. You expend 2 charges and target one creature you can see within 60 feet of yourself, forcing the target to make a DC 17 Wisdom saving throw. On a failed save, the target has the Frightened condition for 1 minute or until it takes damage. While Frightened in this way, the target can do only one of the following on each of its turns: move or take an action or a Bonus Action. If the target is evil, it has Disadvantage on the initial save, and the target can repeat the saving throw at the end of each of its turns, ending the condition on itself on a success.
- Grand Rebuke. You expend 4 charges and target each creature of your choice that you can see within 60 feet of yourself. Each target must succeed on a DC 17 Wisdom saving throw, taking 8d6 Psychic damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one. If the target is evil, it has Disadvantage on the save. 	4989
Emerald Enclave (Go to Renown)
Name	24	Type	Tier	Description	92
Emerald Enclave	5003	Group (Faction)	—	See Forgotten Realms: Heroes of Faerûn for a description, list of Renown rewards, and member benefits. See other sources for additional information.

The Emerald Enclave is a widespread group of wilderness survivalists who preserve the natural order while rooting out unnatural threats. The organization is decentralized, hardy, and reclusive. Barbarians, druids, and rangers of good or neutral alignments are commonly drawn to the Emerald Enclave.

Motto: We preserve the natural order.

Goals
- Restore and preserve the natural order.
- Destroy all that is unnatural.
- Keep the elemental forces of the world in check.
- Keep civilization and the wilderness from destroying each other.

Beliefs
- The natural order must be respected and preserved.
- Forces that upset the natural order must be destroyed.
- Civilization and the wilderness must learn to coexist peacefully.

Ranks
- Springwarden: Renown Score 3+
- Summerstrider: Renown Score 15+
- Autumnreaver: Renown Score 25+
- Winterstalker: Renown Score 40+
- Master of the Wild: Renown Score 50+	5006
Emerald Enclave Clasp (HB)	4895	Wondrous Item	T0	Wondrous Item, T0

This copper cloak clasp is in the shape of a leaf accented with green enamel and serves as faction insignia for new members of the Emerald Enclave. 

While wearing this clasp, you can take a Magic action to determine which way is magnetic north. Nothing happens if the clasp is used in a location that has no magnetic north. Additionally, you can cast the Druidcraft spell from this clasp to predict the weather at your location only.	5013
At Home in the Wild	4895	Story Outcome	T0	Story Outcome, T0

You've shown your commitment and have been taught how to survive in the wild. You have Advantage on ability checks made to calm or befriend Beasts, and you can find food and fresh water for yourself and up to five other people each day, provided that the land offers berries, small game, water, and so forth.	5020
Emerald Cloak (HB)	4895	Wondrous Item	T2 Select	Wondrous Item, T2 (Requires Attunement)

Subtly displayed on the back of this green cloak is the profile of a silhouetted stag, the symbol of the Emerald Enclave. This cloak is presented to members of the Emerald Enclave for demonstrating their relability as faction agents and in carrying out significant duties and tasks.

This cloak has the properties of a Nature's Mantle and you have Advantage on Dexterity (Stealth) checks while wearing this cloak. 

Additionally, while wearing this cloak, you can use a Bonus Action to cast the Tree Stride spell from this cloak except that the spell's duration is 1 round. This property of the cloak can't be used again until the next dawn.	5026
Call the Pack	4895	Story Outcome	T0	Story Outcome, T0

You are a champion of the Emerald Enclave, with your actions recognized to have brought balance and stability. During adventures in Faerûn, you can spend 7 days to call upon major aid from the Emerald Enclave and obtain assistance from Enclave spellcasters or other support. The nature of this assistance is determined by the DM.	5031
Wildmark (HB)	4895	Supernatural Gift	T4 Select	Supernatural Gift, T4

As a Master of the Wild of the Emerald Enclave, you've achieved the highest rank of leadership within the faction and have been inducted into its deepest mysteries. While this Supernatural Gift is active, you gain the following traits.

Nature's Protection. You are immune to curses, disease, and magical contagions and have the benefits of an Amulet of Proof Against Detection and Location. Additionally, you stop aging: you are immune to any effect that would age you, and you can't die from old age.

Nature's Blessing. When you select this Supernatural Gift to be active when finishing a Long Rest, choose one of the following options below to benefit from. You can only benefit from one option at a time.
- Blessing of Eldath. You can cast the Water Breathing spell at will, requiring no components. Additionally, you can cast the Plane Shift spell, requiring no components and targeting yourself only, with the destination being a location on the Elemental Plane of Water. After you cast Plane Shift in this way, you can't do so again until you finish a Long Rest.
- Blessing of Mielikki. You can take a Magic action to shape-shift into a Treant. You retain your game statistics and ability to speak, but your creature type, size, AC, movement modes, Strength, and Dexterity are replaced by those of the Treant stat block, and you gain any statistics and capabilities that the Treant stat block has that you lack. You remain shape-shifted in this way until you are reduced to 0 Hit Points, die, or end it as a Bonus Action. After you shape-shift in this way, you can't do so again until you finish a Short or Long Rest.
- Blessing of Silvanus. You can cast the Meld into Stone spell at will, requiring no components. Additionally, you can cast the Plane Shift spell, requiring no components and targeting yourself only, with the destination being a location on the Elemental Plane of Earth. After you cast Plane Shift in this way, you can't do so again until you finish a Long Rest.	5043
Lords' Alliance (Go to Renown)
Name	24	Type	Tier	Description	92
Lords' Alliance	5057	Group (Faction)	—	See Forgotten Realms: Heroes of Faerûn for a description, list of Renown rewards, and member benefits. See other sources for additional information.

The Lords’ Alliance is a loose coalition of established political powers concerned with mutual security and prosperity. The organization is aggressive, militant, and political. Fighters and sorcerers of lawful or neutral alignments are commonly drawn to the Lords’ Alliance.

Motto: Threats to home must be terminated without prejudice. Superiority is our security. 

Goals
- Ensure the safety and prosperity of cities and other settlements of Faerûn.
- Maintain a strong coalition against the forces of disorder.
- Proactively eliminate threats to the established powers.
- Bring honor and glory to one’s leaders and one’s homeland.

Beliefs
- If civilization is to survive, all must unite against the dark forces that threaten it.
- Fight for your realm. Only you can bring honor, glory, and prosperity to your lord and homeland.
- Don’t wait for the enemy to come to you. The best defense is a strong offense.

Ranks
- Cloak: Renown Score 3+
- Redknife: Renown Score 15+
- Stingblade: Renown Score 25+
- Warduke: Renown Score 40+
- Lioncrown: Renown Score 50+	5069
Signet Ring of the Alliance (HB)	4895	Ring	T0	Ring, T0

Etched upon this signet ring is the shape of a crown, the symbol of the Lords' Alliance. Such rings serve as faction insignia for the Lords' Alliance and are customarily given to newly inducted members and can be dipped in wax to seal important documents.

While wearing this ring, you can take a Magic action to cause your voice or signal to carry clearly for up to 600 feet until the end of your next turn.	5078
Voice of the City	4895	Story Outcome	T0	Story Outcome, T0

You've demonstrated your loyalty and commitment to the lords of the realm. You've been granted training in the civic and legal structures of cities of the Lords' Alliance and cities in general. You have Advantage on Charisma checks made to influence city authorities, city officials, city nobility, and city officers of the law.	5084
Badge of the Alliance (HB)	4895	Wondrous Item	T2 Select	Wondrous Item, T2 (Requires Attunement)

Emblazoned upon this badge is the symbol of a crown, marking one as a ranking member of the Lords' Alliance. These badges are frequently given to Stingblades of the Lords' Alliance after earning the trust of the Lords' Alliance for their actions and marks their authority to act as entrusted agents of the Alliance.

While wearing this badge, you gain a +2 bonus to AC if you aren't using a shield.

Additionally, while wearing this badge, you can cast the Command spell from it (save DC 17).	5093
Assemble the Alliance	4895	Story Outcome	T0	Story Outcome, T0

You are a champion of the Lords' Alliance, with your actions recognized to have brought order and security to the realm. During adventures in Faerûn, you can spend 7 days to call upon major aid from the Lords' Alliance and obtain assistance from Alliance spellcasters or other support. The nature of this assistance is determined by the DM.	5098
Crown of the Alliance (HB)	4895	Wondrous Item	T4 Select	Wondrous Item, T4 (Requires Attunement)

This golden crown is forged in the shape of two rearing lions. This crown is immediately recognizable as a symbol of the Lords' Alliance and is often presented to those that have become a Lioncrown, the highest rank in the leadership of the Lords' Alliance. 

The crown has 7 charges and regains 1d6 + 1 expended charges daily at dawn, and has the following properties.

Resolve. While you wear this crown, you have Resistance to Psychic damage and have Immunity to the Charmed and Frightened conditions. Additionally, each ally within 30 feet of you has Advantage on saving throws made to avoid or end the Charmed or Frightened condition. 

Inspiration. While wearing this crown, you can take a Bonus Action to expend 1 charge from the crown and inspire one creature you can see that is within 30 feet of you and that can see or hear you. Once within the next hour when the inspired creature makes a D20 Test, the inspired creature can roll 1d6 and add the number rolled to the d20. 

Spells. While wearing this crown, you can expend 1 or more of the crown's charges to cast one of the following spells from it: Aid (level 4 version, 4 charges), Circle of Power (5 charges), Crusader's Mantle (3 charges), or Heroism (5 Temporary Hit Points, 1 charge).	5106

Zhentarim	5153	Group (Faction)	—	See Forgotten Realms: Heroes of Faerûn for a description, list of Renown rewards, and member benefits. See other sources for additional information.

The Zhentarim is an unscrupulous shadow network that seeks to expand its influence and power throughout Faerûn. The organization is ambitious, opportunistic, and meritocratic. Rogues and warlocks of neutral and/or evil alignments are commonly drawn to the Zhentarim.

Motto: Join us and prosper. Oppose us and suffer.

Goals
- Amass wealth.
- Look for opportunities to seize power.
- Gain influence over important people and organizations.
- Dominate Faerûn.

Beliefs
- The Zhentarim is your family. You watch out for it, and it watches out for you.
- You are the master of your own destiny. Never be less than what you deserve to be.
- Everything—and everyone—has a price.• The Zhentarim is your family. You watch out for it, and it watches out for you.
- You are the master of your own destiny. Never be less than what you deserve to be.
- Everything—and everyone—has a price.

Ranks

- Wolf: Renown Score 10+
- Viper: Renown Score 25+
- Ardragon: Renown Score 40+
- Dread Lord: Renown Score 50+	5158
Zhentarim Token (HB)	4895	Trinket	T0	Trinket, T0

This iron coin is stamped with the symbol of the Zhentarim, a winged snake biting a coin, and is given to newly inducted members of the Black Network and serves as faction insignia to mark membership within the faction. 

When you display this coin, you have Advantage on Intimidation checks made against merchants, travelers, and others that fear and respect the influence of the Black Network.	5166
Black Network Connections	4895	Story Outcome	T0	Story Outcome, T0

You are a champion of the Zhentarim, with your actions recognized to have brought significant benefit and respect to the Black Network. During adventures in Faerûn, you can spend 7 days to call upon major aid from the Zhentarim and obtain assistance from Zhentarim spellcasters or other support. The nature of this assistance is determined by the DM.	5176
Zhentilar Tattoo (HB)	4895	Wondrous Item (Tattoo)	T2 Select	Wondrous Item (Tattoo), T2

Produced by a special needle, this magic tattoo appears on your body as a Flying Snake which moves about your skin to avoid being seen by others. This tattoo is given to committed members of the Zhentarim, marking their irrevocable dedication to the Black Network for the remainder of their life.

Zhentilar Dedication. To apply this tattoo, you hold the needle to your skin where you want the tattoo to appear, pressing the needle there for duration of a Short Rest. When you finish the Short Rest, the needle turns into the ink that has become the tattoo, which appears on the skin. After the tattoo is applied, it cannot be removed from you unless you die or are targeted with a Wish spell or Divine Intervention, in which case the tattoo vanishes and the needle reappears in your space. 

Tattoo Attunement. You can only attune to this tattoo if it has been applied to you.

Shadowy Acts (Requires Attunement). You have Advantage on Dexterity (Stealth) and Charisma (Intimidation) checks. Additionally, while you are in Dim Light or Darkness, you can Hide as a Bonus Action even if you are being directly observed.

Zhentarim Diplomacy (Requires Attunement). This tattoo has 6 charges and regains 1d6 expended charges daily at dusk. You can expend 1 or more of the tattoo's charges to cast one of the following spells from it (save DC 15): Command (1 charge), Fear (3 charges), or Suggestion (2 charges).	5184
Faceless Eyes of the Zhentarim	4895	Wondrous Item	T4 Select	Wondrous Item, T4 (Requires Attunement)

This stylized hood of black and gold leaves only the eyes visible. This hood is immediately recognizable as a symbol of the Black Network and is often presented to those that have become a Dread Lord, the highest rank in the Zhentarim's leadership.

Faceless Gaze. To gaze upon the faceless eyes of this hood is to invite fear. When a creature starts its turn within 30 feet of you and can see you, you can force it to make a DC 19 Wisdom saving throw if you don't have the Incapacitated condition and can see the creature. On a failed save, the creature has the Frightened condition until the start of its next turn, and if the saving throw fails by 5 or more, the creature's Speed is reduced to 0 while Frightened in this way. Unless surprised, a creature can avert its eyes to avoid the saving throw at the start of its next turn. If the creature does so, it can't see you until the start of its next turn, when it can avert its eyes again. If the creature looks at you in the meantime, it must immediately make the save.

Mark for Death. You can take a Magic action to choose one creature you can see within 30 feet of you, marking it until the end of your next turn. The next time you deal damage to the marked creature, the creature has Vulnerability to all of that source's damage, and then the mark ends.	5193

# Item Properties

Jump to
Magic Item Special Features	2422
Creator	History	Minor Property	Quirk	Sentient Magic Items
Any Item Properties listed as Fair Game are available to be awarded by any DM. Any Item Properties listed as Full DM Only can only be awarded by Full DMs (or via #dm-collaboration with a Full DM)
Name	24	Type	Category	Description	92
Magic Item Special Features
A magic item can have Special Features as described in the 2014 or 2024 Dungeon Master's Guide for loot awarded by a DM as logged in #treasure-log and #session-log
A magic item can have up to 1 Creator, up to 1 History, up to 2 Minor Properties, and up to 1 Quirk in this way
If a Special Feature grants a numerical bonus, you can benefit from only one bonus of the same kind that adds to the same value. For example, if you have two magic items with the Guardian minor property, you only benefit from one of them, not both.

Sentient Magic Items
Any magic item can be a sentient magic item using the rules in the 2014 or 2024 Dungeon Master's Guide for loot awarded by a DM as logged in #treasure-log and #session-logs 
Only Full DMs (or DMs collaborating with one via #dm-collaboration) can award sentient magic items in this way.
Existing examples of sentient magic items present in the 2014 or 2024 Dungeon Master's Guide can be referenced for how to format the Sentience and Personality properties of a sentient magic item.

Fair Game	3408
Core	Supplemental	Adventures	Non-FR	4493	Supplemental	Adventures	Non-FR
Except with a DM's permission during an adventure, only monsters in Fair Game as printed can be used by PCs for purposes of spells and game features that let them summon or shape-shift into a creature. 
Statblocks of unique NPCs and monster variants, even if reflavored, cannot be used in this way except with a DM's permission during an adventure.
DMs should take care to review the statblock, lore, and Notes / Rage Advice for any monster they plan to include in a game.

Full DM Only
Full DM Only monsters can only be used by Full DMs in adventures they run, regardless of if they show up in a non-combat or combat capacity. Any major named NPC is considered to be a Full DM Only monster.
The use of Full DM Only monsters in combat should avoid sweeping changes, especially those that would prevent their use by other DMs, without collaborating with other DMs and Lore.

# Backgrounds

Backgrounds
Characters on the server can originate from the Forgotten Realms or broader D&D 5e multiverse, which includes the different D&D planes of existence and other worlds of the Material Plane corresponding to campaign settings that have official or approved D&D 5e source material (e.g. Eberron, Dragonlance, Greyhawk, Humblewood, etc). Originating from another world or setting requires a request to the Lore Consultants.

A Pact of the Chain warlock can't summon Pact of the Chain familiars with the spell.
- Galder's Speedy Courier: After receiving a chest, the target can block your ability to reach it again with this spell for 8 hours. If you try to send another chest during that time, you learn you are blocked, and the spell fails.

Immovable Object: The target must be an object that isn't being worn or carried.
Spells from IDROF	Icewind Dale: Rime of the Frostmaiden	Create Magen is Wizard only, and Frost Fingers is Sorcerer, Warlock,  Wizard.

All Wizard spells are present in the ----- Community Spellbook	Create Magen: The hitpoint maximum reduction also is removed if the magen dies. A PC can only have 1 existing magen.
Spells from TCE	Tasha's Cauldron of Everything	All Wizard spells are present in the ----- Community Spellbook	- Booming Blade: The cost requirement for the weapon is removed, the spell's range is the weapon's reach (not Self), and the spell targets a creature within the weapon's reach. The spell can be used with War Caster.
- Dream of the Blue Veil: The material component can't be substitued with a component pouch or spellcasting focus.
- Green-Flame Blade: The cost requirement for the weapon is removed, the spell's range is the weapon's reach (not Self), and the spell targets a creature within the weapon's reach. The spell can be used with War Caster.
- Lightning Lure: The spell's range is 15 feet (not Self) and targets all other creatures in range. The spell can be used with War Caster.
- Sword Burst: The spell's range is 5 feet (not Self) and targets all other creatures in range. The spell can be used with War Caster.
- Summon Aberration/Beast/Celestial/Construct/Elemental/Fey/Fiend/Shadowspawn/Undead: The summoned creature has a number of Hit Dice equal to the spell level (d6 HD for Small, d8 HD for Medium, and d10 HD for Large)
Spells from FTD	Fizban's Treasury of Dragons	All Wizard spells are present in the ----- Community Spellbook
Spells from SCC	Strixhaven: A Curriculum of Chaos	All Wizard spells are present in the ----- Community Spellbook	- Silvery Barbs: The reroll affects the final result of the original roll, including after any advantage or disadvantage were applied. A creature chosen to receive advantage from the spell is a target of the spell; therefore, the spell can't benefit from the Twinned Spell Metamagic, but it can benefit from the Split Enchantment class feature if no creature is granted advantage.
- Vortex Warp: A chosen surface must be able to support the target; you can't use the spell to force a creature to fall.
Spells from AAG	Astral Adventurer's Guide	Air Bubble is present in ----- Community Spellbook. Create Spelljamming Helm is not in the ----- Community Spellbook.	A spelljamming helm can only be installed onto an air, water, or space vehicle listed in Equipment except for a Canoe or Rowboat. Additionally, a spelljamming helm created by Create Spelljamming Helm cannot be sold.
Spells from BMT	The Book of Many Things	All Wizard spells are present in the ----- Community Spellbook
Spells from SatO	Sigil and the Outlands	All Wizard spells are present in the ----- Community Spellbook
Spells from HWCS	Humblewood Campaign Setting	All Wizard spells are present in the ----- Community Spellbook	- Globe of Twilight: Has a range of Self (15-foot radius, 15 feet high)
- Invoke the Amaranthine: Can call upon any deity or otherworldly being, not just an Amaranthine of Everden
Spells from HWT	Humblewood Tales	Only Mend Plants is added. Other spells are reprints of what is available in Humblewood Campaign Setting (see above).
Spells from PHB (2024)	Player's Handbook (2024)	Can only be used by 2024 characters.

- Befuddlement replaces Feeblemind from PHB 2014.
- Find Steed also supersedes Find Greater Steed from XGTE.
- Shining Smite replaces Branding Smite from PHB 2014.
- Summon Dragon is the renamed version of Summon Draconic Spirit from FTD, but a 2024 character can use either version of the spell.

Refer to the PHB 2024 Spell Changes for more details.

All Wizard spells are present in the ----- Community Spellbook	- Awaken: can't be used by PCs to increase their Intelligence or gain languages.
- Clone: A creature can have only 1 existing clone, with both caster and target spending 128 DTP (if you are both the caster and target, it is 128 DTP total).
- Contingency: can't be stored into a Glyph of Warding.
- Fabricate: The raw materials can be bought in downtime and cost the full cost of the item.
- Find Familiar: In addition to the listed options, the following options can be used: Blood Hawk, Flying Snake
- Gate: A creature named by the spell knows it is being targeted by the spell and can choose to refuse being summoned, causing the spell to fail.
- Glyph of Warding: can't be cast into the extradimensional space of a bag of holding or other magic items that create extradimensional spaces.
- Magic Jar: can't be used to permanently swap bodies.
- Planar Binding: A PC can only have 1 existing bound creature.
- Reincarnate: The body part used in the spell must be taken from after the target has died and can include blood. A reincarnated PC can swap any racial feats they no longer qualify for with another feat or ASI. A PC reincarnated into a human gains an additional Origin feat. A PC reincarnated out of human loses their racial Origin feat. 
- Simulacrum: Only 1 simulacrum can exist per caster.
- Symbol: can't be cast into the extradimensional space of a bag of holding or other magic items that create extradimensional spaces.
- Animate Objects, Giant Insect, Summon Aberration / Beast / Celestial / Construct / Dragon / Elemental / Fey / Fiend / Undead: The summoned creature has a number of Hit Dice equal to the spell level (d6 HD for Small, d8 HD for Medium, and d10 HD for Large)
- Tasha's Bubbling Cauldron: creates the caster's choice of a number of T1 or lower potions.These potions can't be traded.
- Teleportation Circle: PCs know the following sigil sequences: -----, Durlag's Tower, Lerwick Outpost, Merkin's Keep, Port Nyanzaru, Suzail, Silent Tower, Tinkerton
- Teleport: can't be used in downtime except to a permanent circle or a safe location you possess a linked object for
- True Polymorph: Any permanent transformations are reverted at the end of a DM's adventure, except a a PC transformed as a type of character retirement.
- Wish: Using the spell to Reshape Reality requires approval from the Lore Consultants and/or Rule Architects, unless the effect is limited to the scope of a DM's adventure. If you receive the effect of a Wish from other than a PC or a PC-controlled creature, its effect is limited to the scope of a DM's adventure except as approved by the Lore Consultants and/or Rule Architects. A character that loses the ability to cast the spell can never regain the ability to do so, even by reworking, except only by Roll Redo from another Wish spell or Heroic Inspiration. A PC-controlled creature casting this spell (such as a simulacrum) can only use it to duplicate a spell as written. Additionally, a creature only benefits from one instance of the Resistance option at a time; if the spell is used again to confer Resistance, only the most recent casting applies.
Spells from FRHOF (2024)	Forgotten Realms: Heroes of Faerûn (2024)	Can only be used by 2024 characters.

Circle Magic can be used as written with any eligible spell in Allowed Content (Spells)

Deryan's Helpful Homunculi and Conjure Constructs are also Artificer spells for the 2024 EFOTA Artificer

All Wizard spells except the following are present in the ----- Community Spellbook: Backlash, Conjure Constructs, and Spellfire Storm	- Deryan's Helpful Homunculi: The assistant created by this spell can assist with crafting either Equipment or in crafting magic items. If the assistant is used to help craft Equipment, the DTP cost to craft that Equipment is halved (rounded up). However, you must pay for the spell’s Material component costs for each DTP spent this way to craft and additionally add the spell level this spell is cast at in DTP. For crafting magic items, the assistant cannot be used to assist in crafting any magic items that can be used to cast spells or that have restricted attunement requirements. The assistant otherwise can be used to halve the base DTP cost to craft a magic item (rounded up) at your Bastion as if they were an assisting PC. However, you must pay for the spell’s Material component costs for each DTP spent this way to craft and additionally add the spell level this spell is cast at in DTP, as well as the additional 3 or 21 DTP as normal. The assistant can be used in conjunction with an assisting PC. The DTP cost to craft a magic item in this way is two-thirds of its original base DTP cost, with you and the assisting PC expending one-third of the base DTP cost each (round up). However, you must pay for the spell’s Material component costs for each DTP you spent this way to craft and additionally add the spell level this spell is cast at in DTP, as well as the additional 3 or 21 DTP as normal.
- Dirge: The Speed of a target that succeeds on its saving throw is halved until the end of the creature's next turn.
- Doomtide: The spell’s special component (a string of three black pearls from Pandemonium) that is used when casting the spell as a circle spell can’t be obtained in downtime but instead typically from an adventure.
Spells from EFOTA (2024)	Eberron: Forge of the Artificer (2024)

Mounts, Vehicles, and Pets
Mounts, Vehicles, and Pets can be resold for half price.
Jump to
Mounts	Vehicles	Pets
Mounts (Go to Mounts, Vehicles, and Pets)
A mount's participation in an adventure is at the DM's discretion.
A DM can use the guidelines on customizing monsters in the DM Guidelines for mounts they award.
A DM can choose to award a mount that has been awakened (such as by the Awaken spell) but the pet is then considered to be a sapient mount.
A sapient mount's name, alignment, and personality must be written in the session log in which it is acquired.
Sapient mounts cannot be traded or sold.

Pets (Go to Mounts, Vehicles, and Pets)
Pets can't take the Attack or Help actions and normally can't participate in adventures: a pet only participates in adventures otherwise with a DM's express permission and at their discretion
A DM can submit a Rules request to award a creature not listed here as a vanity pet
Pets can also be awarded as the equivalent of a T0 Permanent item and follow the associated item distribution rules for doing so 
A DM can use the guidelines on customizing monsters in the DM Guidelines for pets they award.
Sapient pets cannot be traded or sold.                                                
A sapient pet's name, alignment, and personality must be written in the session log in which it is acquired.
A DM can choose to award a pet that has been awakened (such as by the Awaken spell) but the pet is then considered to be a sapient pet.

# Downtime

Bastions	Buying and Selling	Crafting	Research	Reworking	Spellcasting	Spellcasting Services	Trading	Training	Traveling	Work	Miscellaneous
You can't spent the same DTP for more than one activity. Except where otherwise stated, the minimum DTP for any activity is 1.
You additionally expend 1 GP per DTP expended on activities other than Traveling while not in -----
Name of Activity	2130	DTP Cost	Description	92
Bastions
You must be level 5+ to build and benefit from a Bastion. You can only build and benefit from one Bastion as part of downtime (see Appendix B of the TBD Guidelines for details)
You can only construct a Bastion either a) near ----- or a guild outpost or b) at another world location if you are able to claim a parcel of land or stronghold as part of an adventure as determined by the DM.
Name of Activity	2130	DTP Cost	Description	92
Construct or Expand a Bastion	2177	Varies	Constructing or expanding a Bastion by adding or replacing one or more facilities. See the Bastions sheet for details and Appendix B of the TBD Guidelines for details.	2189
Construct or Expand a Shared Bastion	2177	Varies	Constructing or expanding a Bastion that is Shared by characters of different players.* See the Bastions sheet for details and Appendix B of the TBD Guidelines for details.**

* Each participant must build one or more Special Facilities of a Shared Bastion, with the maximum number of Special Facilities in a Shared Bastion according to the highest level participating PC (up to 6 by level 17+).

** An existing Bastion can be converted into a Shared Bastion with new participants constructing one or more Special Facilities. If the Bastion already has the maximum number of Special Facilities according to the highest level participating PC, an existing Special Facility must be torn down first to make room. Additionally, Shared Bastions can be Combined with either the singular or Shared Bastions of PCs of other players as normal.	2214

Reworking
All reworks must be logged in #character-rework-log for approval by an Auditor
You must log all reworks in your Master Adventurer Log (MAL) in the session section
You can fully refund any rework prior to your first game played or DMPCed with that character. Log the refund in #character-rework-log for approval by an Auditor
You can partially refund (50% GP and DTP recouped) any rework before your 3rd game played or DMPCed with that character. Log the refund in #character-rework-log for approval by an Auditor

Trading
Only PCs in the same location can trade with each other and any items you obtain via trading with another PC must be of your PC's tier or lower
You can't trade gold or equipment that can be sold for gold between your PCs, even by proxy
Items and equpment created by class features can't be traded or sold. Supernatural Rewards also cannot be traded or sold.

Training
Besides learning by yourself, another player's PC that knows a language or has a tool proficiency can teach your PC that language or tool proficiency. You expend the listed DTP cost but no gold and the teacher PC expends half the listed DTP.
Both your PC and the teacher PC must be in the same location, and the teacher must be another player's PC; the teacher cannot be one of your own PCs.
The teacher can choose at their discretion to charge you gold for teaching, using the rules for trading gold.
